- **research-framework-figure**: 研究架構圖（概念模型圖）產生器。把研究假說畫成投稿與口試等級的架構圖：中介模型 X→M→Y、調節模型、被調節的中介、序列中介，以及台灣商管論文最常用的『中介＋雙控制變數框』版式（每個依變數各一組控制變數，以虛線彎箭頭接入）。輸入一份 JSON 規格，輸出可再編輯的向量圖：SVG（零依賴、可用 Illustrator/Inkscape 編修、可直接插入 Word）與 PPTX（原生圖形，口試前可在 PowerPoint 內直接改字改框）。內建字型紀律：中文標楷體、英數 Times New Roman，並在字型缺漏時誠實告知而非假裝已套用。何時用：論文第三章要畫研究架構圖、口試簡報要放概念模型、假說路徑圖要重畫或改版、審稿人要求補架構圖時。觸發詞：研究架構圖、架構圖、概念模型、概念性架構、研究模型圖、假說圖、路徑圖、中介模型圖、調節模型圖、被調節的中介、moderated mediation、序列中介、mediation diagram、conceptual model、conceptual framework、研究框架、變數關係圖、H1 H2 H3 圖、畫模型圖、模型示意圖、控制變數框。另支援 sample_flow 版式：PRISMA 流程圖、樣本篩選流程圖、樣本刪減圖、樣本流程、attrition。與 management-figure 劃界：那個畫『統計結果圖』（係數森林圖、… ([Nero1688/claude-academic-skills](https://github.com/Nero1688/claude-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/research-framework-figure) · ★13 · 3 copies)

- **research-guardian**: AI 研究品質防護系統。在 AI agent 執行研究任務時，自動進行多重驗證、事實查核、文獻交叉比對、實驗設計檢查，防止幻覺、邏輯錯誤、引用錯誤、與 novelty 誤判。 任何時候 AI 被要求進行以下操作都應觸發本 skill： 生成研究假設、撰寫文獻回顧、設計實驗、分析數據、撰寫論文段落、評估研究結果的 novelty、 進行 systematic review、做 meta-analysis、提出研究方向建議、或任何聲稱「發現」或「創新」的場景。 即使使用者沒有明確要求驗證，只要涉及研究性質的輸出，都應主動啟動防護流程。 本 skill 設計用於整合進 AI agent pipeline，作為研究輸出的品質閘門。 ([htlin222/research-guardian-skill](https://github.com/htlin222/research-guardian-skill/tree/HEAD/research-guardian) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **research-guardrail**: Guardrail for AI-assisted research and analytical/computational work in ANY field — data analysis, statistics, machine learning, simulations, modeling, quantitative finance, computational science, and similar. (1) '리서치해줘', '깊게 조사해줘', '근거와 함께 정리해줘', '팩트체크된 보고서' 요청 시, (2) '여러 출처로 검증해줘', '인용 달아서 조사' 요청 시, (3) 후속으로 '다시 조사', '재실행', '더 깊게', '소스 추가해서 보완', '이 주제만 다시' 요청 시 반드시 사용. 단순 단일 사실 질문(직접 답변 가능)에는 트리거하지 않는다. - **research-idea-gate**: S0 intake + G0 kill test for a research idea or a research pivot, before anything is built. ([emanualjade/strike](https://github.com/emanualjade/strike/tree/HEAD/plugins/strike/skills/research-initiative))
- **research-innovation** ([P-Core-System/pcore-orchestra](https://github.com/P-Core-System/pcore-orchestra/tree/HEAD/bundle/opencode/skills/research-innovation))
- **research-innovation**: Research design, experimentation, technology scouting, prototyping, and innovation management. Use when designing research experiments, evaluating new technologies or vendors, building proof of concepts, or planning R&D portfolios. ([P-Core-System/pcore-orchestra](https://github.com/P-Core-System/pcore-orchestra/tree/HEAD/bundle/skills/research-innovation))
- **research-innovation-explorer**: Build literature-grounded research-question candidate landscapes by collecting papers, generating A+B matrices, and dynamically reviewing combinations with traceable evidence, uncertainty, and next checks. Use when an AI agent needs to explore a field, screen research questions, compare paper combinations, inspect prior art, or prepare a provisional shortlist for researcher review. ([foryourhealth111-pixel/research-innovation-explorer](https://github.com/foryourhealth111-pixel/research-innovation-explorer/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★86)
- **research-innvtn-explorer**: Explore literature-grounded research innovation ideas and paper framing in a host-neutral way. Use when an AI agent needs to collect a recent paper pool, run broad and deep literature search, decompose methods into reusable capabilities, generate A+B or module-combination candidates, shortlist feasible ideas, design a defensible unifying framework, and produce an elegant Markdown report with citations, analysis rationale, and visual summaries. ([Undermybelt/hermes-skills](https://github.com/Undermybelt/hermes-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/research/research-innvtn-explorer) · ★11)
- **research-inquiry**: Create a detailed section outline with RESEARCH directives for a topic file. Use after inception to structure a stub topic before investigation. Argument: topic file path relative to research/content/. ([mtrense/skills](https://github.com/mtrense/skills/tree/HEAD/research/skills/research-inquiry) · ★9)
- **research-inquiry-cycle**: Drive all stub topics in the research project to `inquiry` status by spawning research-inquiry-worker subagents in parallel batches — one worker per stub topic, since inquiry never collides at the topic-file level. ([mtrense/skills](https://github.com/mtrense/skills/tree/HEAD/research/skills/research-inquiry-cycle) · ★9)
- **research-insight-critic**: Critical, project-grounded research collaboration for科研 insight, storytelling, algorithm design, research idea formulation, reviewer objections, ablation planning, experiment design, and项目内科研讨论. Use when the user asks to梳理, formulate, brainstorm, pressure-test, critique, refine, or turn a research thought into a rigorous claim, especially with local project docs, related paper recaps, robotics, embodied AI, reinforcement learning, dexterous manipulation, world models, Sim-to-Real, tactile/contact, or actuator dynamics. ([kryang03/Notes](https://github.com/kryang03/Notes/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/research-insight-critic) · ★4)
- **research-insight-log**: 研究灵感日志：记录“我突然想到”“记一下这个想法”的hypothesis/analogy/research-question/method-idea/experiment-idea等，绑定触发来源(source_ids/note_ids/context)、potential value与验证问题。Use when users ask to capture hunches/ideas not yet proven, for later validation instead of direct claims. ([kylecui/petfish.ai](https://github.com/kylecui/petfish.ai/tree/HEAD/packs/optional/research-skill-pack/.opencode/skills/research-insight-log) · ★1)
